167766_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = character:167766
			exists = character:190457
		}
		character:167766 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:190457 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}

			exists = character:everstein_1
		}
		character:everstein_3 = { save_scope_as = awow_child}
		character:everstein_1 = { save_scope_as = awow_other_mother}
		awow_assign_historic_father_as_other_mother = yes
		clear_saved_scope = awow_child
		clear_saved_scope = awow_other_mother
	}
}
